# Summary

Every failed request returns an HTTP 4xx or 5xx status and a JSON body in the failure envelope (see [Response envelope](response-envelope.md)):

```json
{
  "status": "failure",
  "summary": "SECURITY_NOT_PERMITTED",
  "data": {
    "errorCode": 7301,
    "errorMessage": "You (<user-name>) do not have the permission to do this operation. "
  }
}
```

How to use this catalog:

- Match on `data.errorCode` (integer), never on `errorMessage` text, which is localized and may change.
- `summary` carries a stable upper-case constant for many codes; the known constants are listed per code below.
- The HTTP status column says "observed" when a documented sample response shows the status, and "typical" when it is inferred from the code family (401 for token errors, 403 for permission errors, 404 for missing objects, 500 for server errors, 400 for everything else).
- The same code can carry an operation-specific meaning; the "Raised by" table lists each documented reason and solution per operation.
- Retry guidance: 5xx and concurrency guards (for example a job or copy already in progress) are retryable after a delay; 4xx codes are not retryable without changing the request.

# Code Families

| Range | Family | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| 6xxx | Organization, user and role management | Membership, roles, portal domains. |
| 7xxx | Metadata, security and modeling | 7103/7104 missing objects, 7301 permission denied, 73xx sharing, 74xx query tables and data lock, 75xx criteria and formula validation, 78xx export/import. |
| 8xxx | Request validation, jobs and platform limits | 80xx invalid CONFIG and parameters, 81xx import/export jobs and limits, 82xx system tags, 85xx OAuth and parameter framework. |
| 9xxx, 12xxx, 14xxx, 15xxx, 18xxx, 70xxx, 101xxx | Feature specific | Localization (9102), white label (12xxx), scheduling and datasource sync (18xxx), AutoML (70xxx), stream tables (101021). |

| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |

## Error 7104

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7104` |
| Summary constant | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` |
| HTTP status | 404 (typical) |
| Meaning | The view (table, report, dashboard, query table) or other named object addressed by the request does not exist in the given workspace. |
| Resolution | Verify the view-id or the exact, case-sensitive name used in the request. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Get Meta Details From Name](../domains/organization-management/org-info-and-settings/get-meta-details.md) | Workspace or view not found by the provided name. | Verify the exact spelling and case of `workspaceName` and `viewName`. Names are case-sensitive. |
| [Export as Template](../domains/workspace-management/workspace-operations/export-as-template.md) | One or more specified view IDs do not exist. | Verify all `viewIds` are valid using the Get View List API. |
| [Add Row](../domains/data-operations/row-operations/add-row.md), [Batch Import Data into Existing Table](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/batch-import-existing-table.md), [Create Import Job for an Existing Table (Asynchronous)](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/create-import-job-existing-table.md), [Delete Row](../domains/data-operations/row-operations/delete-rows.md), [Import Data into an Existing Table (Synchronous)](../domains/data-operations/sync-data-import/import-data-existing-table.md), [Update Row](../domains/data-operations/row-operations/update-rows.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — The view does not exist. | Verify `<view-id>`. |
| [Add Multiple Tags To View](../domains/views-management/tags/add-tags-to-view.md), [Create Export Job using View ID (Asynchronous)](../domains/data-operations/async-data-export/create-export-job-view-id.md), [Export Data from a View](../domains/data-operations/sync-data-export/export-data-view.md), [Get Last Import Details](../domains/data-operations/data-sync-and-connectivity/get-last-import-details.md), [Get View Tags](../domains/views-management/tags/get-view-tags.md), [Refetch Data](../domains/data-operations/data-sync-and-connectivity/refetch-datasource.md), [Remove Multiple Tags From View](../domains/views-management/tags/remove-tags-from-view.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — The view does not exist. | Verify `<view-id>` with [Get View List](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-views.md). |
| [Save As View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/save-as-view.md) | Source view not found. | Verify `<view-id>` exists within the specified workspace. |
| [Copy Views](../domains/views-management/view-operations/copy-views.md) | One or more view IDs in `viewIds` do not exist. | Verify each view ID in the array exists and is accessible. |
| [Create Similar Views](../domains/views-management/view-operations/create-similar-views.md) | Target table (`<view-id>`) or reference table (`referenceViewId`) not found. | Verify both view IDs exist in the workspace. |
| [Add Favourite View](../domains/views-management/view-preferences/add-favorite-view.md), [Add Multiple Tags To View](../domains/views-management/tags/add-tags-to-view.md), [Delete View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/delete-view.md), [Get View Dependents](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-dependents.md), [Get View Details](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-details.md), [Get View Tags](../domains/views-management/tags/get-view-tags.md), [Get View URL](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-url.md), [Remove Favourite View](../domains/views-management/view-preferences/remove-favorite-view.md), [Remove Multiple Tags From View](../domains/views-management/tags/remove-tags-from-view.md), [Rename View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/rename-view.md) | View not found. | Verify `<view-id>` exists in the workspace. |
| [Add Favourite View](../domains/views-management/view-preferences/add-favorite-view.md), [Add Multiple Tags To View](../domains/views-management/tags/add-tags-to-view.md), [Delete View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/delete-view.md), [Get View Dependents](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-dependents.md), [Get View Details](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-details.md), [Get View Tags](../domains/views-management/tags/get-view-tags.md), [Get View URL](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-url.md), [Remove Favourite View](../domains/views-management/view-preferences/remove-favorite-view.md), [Remove Multiple Tags From View](../domains/views-management/tags/remove-tags-from-view.md), [Rename View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/rename-view.md) | View not found. | Verify `<view-id>` is valid and accessible. |
| [Add Favourite View](../domains/views-management/view-preferences/add-favorite-view.md), [Add Multiple Tags To View](../domains/views-management/tags/add-tags-to-view.md), [Delete View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/delete-view.md), [Get View Dependents](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-dependents.md), [Get View Details](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-details.md), [Get View Tags](../domains/views-management/tags/get-view-tags.md), [Get View URL](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-view-url.md), [Remove Favourite View](../domains/views-management/view-preferences/remove-favorite-view.md), [Remove Multiple Tags From View](../domains/views-management/tags/remove-tags-from-view.md), [Rename View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/rename-view.md) | View not found. | Verify the `<view-id>` exists in the specified workspace. |

| [Delete Embed URL](../domains/share-and-publish/embed-url/delete-embed-url.md), [Fetch All Embed URLs](../domains/share-and-publish/embed-url/get-embed-urls.md), [Get Embed URL](../domains/share-and-publish/embed-url/get-embed-url.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — View not found. | Verify `<view-id>` exists in the workspace. |
| [Create Email Schedule](../domains/schedules-and-alerts/email-schedules/create-email-schedule.md), [Create Slide Show](../domains/share-and-publish/slideshow-management/create-slideshow.md), [Update Slide Show](../domains/share-and-publish/slideshow-management/update-slideshow.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — A view in `viewIds` does not exist. | Verify the IDs via [Get View List](../domains/views-management/view-operations/get-views.md). |
| [Get Meta Details From Name](../domains/organization-management/org-info-and-settings/get-meta-details.md) | Workspace or view not found for the provided name. | Verify the exact spelling and case of workspaceName and viewName. The names are case sensitive. |
| [Export as Template](../domains/workspace-management/workspace-operations/export-as-template.md) | One or more of the specified view IDs do not exist. | Verify that all the values in viewIds are valid using the Get View List API. |
| [Create Similar Views](../domains/views-management/view-operations/create-similar-views.md) | The target table given as view-id, or the reference table given as referenceViewId, was not found. | Verify that both view IDs exist in the workspace. |

## Error 7105

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7105` |
| Summary constant | Not documented |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | The specified view does not exist. |
| Resolution | Verify the view-id using the Get View List API. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Get Table Metadata](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/table-and-schema/get-table-metadata.md) | The specified view does not exist. | Verify the `<view-id>` using the Get View List API. |

## Error 7106

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7106` |
| Summary constant | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` |
| HTTP status | 404 (observed in sample responses) |
| Meaning | The schedule has no surviving views to send. |
| Resolution | Recreate the schedule against existing views. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Trigger Email Schedule](../domains/schedules-and-alerts/email-schedules/trigger-email-schedule.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — The schedule has no surviving views to send. | Recreate the schedule against existing views. |

## Error 7107

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7107` |
| Summary constant | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` |
| HTTP status | 400 (observed in sample responses) |
| Meaning | The specified column does not exist in the table. |
| Resolution | Verify the column-id using the Get Table Metadata API. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Delete Column](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/delete-column.md), [Get Column Dependents](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/get-column-dependents.md), [Rename Column](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/rename-column.md) | The specified column does not exist in the table. | Verify the `<column-id>` using Get Table Metadata. |
| [Delete Column](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/delete-column.md), [Get Column Dependents](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/get-column-dependents.md), [Rename Column](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/rename-column.md) | The specified column does not exist in the table. | Verify `<column-id>` using Get Table Metadata. |
| [Hide Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/hide-columns.md), [Show Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/show-columns.md) | One or more column IDs do not exist in the table. | Verify all IDs using Get Table Metadata. |
| [Add Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/add-lookup.md) | The specified child column (`<column-id>`) does not exist in the child table. | Verify `<column-id>` using Get Table Metadata on the child view. |
| [Remove Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/remove-lookup.md) | The specified child column (`<column-id>`) does not exist in the child table. | Verify `<column-id>` using Get Table Metadata. |
| [Delete Custom Formula](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/formula-columns/delete-formula-column.md) | The specified column ID does not exist in the view. | Verify `<formula-id>` using Get Custom Formulas. |
| [Delete Aggregate Formula](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/aggregate-formulas/delete-aggregate-formula.md) | The specified formula ID does not exist as a column on this view. | Verify `<formula-id>` using Get Aggregate Formula. |
| [Create Import Job for an Existing Table (Asynchronous)](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/create-import-job-existing-table.md), [Import Data into an Existing Table (Synchronous)](../domains/data-operations/sync-data-import/import-data-existing-table.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — A column named in `matchingColumns` is not present in the table. | Verify the names via [Get Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/overview.md). |
| [Batch Import Data into Existing Table](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/batch-import-existing-table.md) | `META_OBJECT_NOT_PRESENT` — A column named in `matchingColumns` is not present. | Verify the names via [Get Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/overview.md). |
| [Auto Analyse Column](../domains/views-management/auto-analysis/auto-analyse-column.md) | Column not found in the specified table. | Verify `<column-id>` belongs to the table identified by `<view-id>`. |

## Error 7111

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |

| [Rename Column](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/rename-column.md) | A column with the new name already exists in the table. | Use a unique column name within the table. |

| [Batch Import Data into New Table](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/batch-import-new-table.md) | `META_DBOBJECT_NAME_DUPLICATED` — An object with this `tableName` already exists. | Choose a different name, or use [Batch Import Data into Existing Table](../domains/data-operations/async-data-import/batch-import-existing-table.md). |
| [Rename View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/rename-view.md), [Save As View](../domains/views-management/view-operations/save-as-view.md) | A view with the given `viewName` already exists in this workspace. | Choose a different, unique name for the new view. |

## Error 7307

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7307` |
| Summary constant | `OWNER_CANNOT_SHARE_HIMSELF` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | The sharer attempted to share a view to themselves. |
| Resolution | Remove the sharer's own email from emailIds. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Share Views](../domains/share-and-publish/sharing/share-views.md) | `OWNER_CANNOT_SHARE_HIMSELF` — The sharer attempted to share a view to themselves. | Remove the sharer's own email from `emailIds`. |

## Error 7309

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7309` |
| Summary constant | `SECURITY_NEEDS_LOGIN` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | No authentication was supplied with the request. |
| Resolution | Send the Authorization header carrying a valid OAuth token. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Create Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/create-custom-role.md), [Delete Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/delete-custom-role.md), [Get Custom Roles](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/get-custom-roles.md), [Update Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/update-custom-role.md) | `SECURITY_NEEDS_LOGIN` — No authentication was supplied. | Send an `Authorization` header. |
| [Create Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/create-custom-role.md), [Delete Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/delete-custom-role.md), [Get Custom Roles](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/get-custom-roles.md), [Update Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/update-custom-role.md) | No authentication was supplied with the request. | Send the Authorization header carrying a valid OAuth token. |

## Error 7319

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `7319` |
| Summary constant | `OBJID_NOT_BELONGS_TO_DB` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | The view does not belong to the specified workspace. |
| Resolution | Confirm that the view-id belongs to the workspace identified by workspace-id. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Export as Template](../domains/workspace-management/workspace-operations/export-as-template.md) | One or more specified view IDs do not belong to the specified workspace. | Ensure all `viewIds` belong to the workspace identified by `<workspace-id>`. |
| [Move Views To Folder](../domains/workspace-management/workspace-folders/move-views-to-folder.md) | One or more view IDs do not belong to the specified workspace. | Ensure all view IDs in `viewIds` belong to the workspace identified by `<workspace-id>`. |
| [Get Table Metadata](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/table-and-schema/get-table-metadata.md) | The specified view ID does not belong to the given workspace. | Confirm the `<view-id>` belongs to the workspace identified by `<workspace-id>`. |
| [Get Column Dependents](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/get-column-dependents.md), [Reorder Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/reorder-columns.md), [Sort Data by Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/sort-data-by-columns.md) | The view ID does not belong to the specified workspace. | Confirm the `<view-id>` belongs to the workspace in the URL. |
| [Get Column Dependents](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/get-column-dependents.md), [Reorder Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/reorder-columns.md), [Sort Data by Columns](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/columns/sort-data-by-columns.md) | The view ID does not belong to the specified workspace. | Confirm the `<view-id>` is correct. |
| [Add Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/add-lookup.md) | The reference table (`referenceViewId`) does not belong to the same workspace as the child table. | Both tables must be in the workspace identified by `<workspace-id>`. |
| [Remove Lookup](../domains/data-modeling-and-schema/lookups-and-relationships/remove-lookup.md) | The child table (`<view-id>`) does not belong to the specified workspace. | Confirm `<view-id>` is a table within `<workspace-id>`. |

## Error 8539

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `8539` |
| Summary constant | `INVALID_VALUE_NOT_ALLOWED` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | An attribute carries a value that is structurally valid but not accepted, such as an empty roleName. |
| Resolution | Send a non-empty, valid value. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:

| Operation(s) | Reason | Solution |
|---|---|---|
| [Create Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/create-custom-role.md) | `INVALID_VALUE_NOT_ALLOWED` — An attribute carries a value that is structurally valid but not accepted, such as an empty `roleName`. | Send a non-empty value. |
| [Create Custom Role](../domains/users-and-groups/custom-roles/create-custom-role.md) | An attribute carries a value that is structurally valid but not accepted, such as an empty roleName. | Send a non-empty, valid value. |

## Error 8544

| Attribute | Value |
|---|---|
| Error code | `8544` |
| Summary constant | `OUT_OF_RANGE` |
| HTTP status | 400 (typical) |
| Meaning | A schedule value is outside its declared range. |
| Resolution | Correct the value. |
| Retryable | No, fix the request first |

Raised by:
